review_text: We had such a great experience this morning at Island Pancake House! Our waiter, Chin, was absolutely amazing. He was so polite, attentive, and made sure our family was well taken care of the entire time.

One thing that really stood out to us was that they were playing worship music this Sunday morning. As a Christian household, that meant a lot to us since we missed church this morning to spend time together as a family. When we stopped to pray over our food, Chin patiently waited for us to finish before speaking so he wouldn’t interrupt our prayer, and that small act of respect honestly meant so much.

The food was incredible too. Everything was seasoned perfectly, tasted fresh, and the portions were huge for the price. You definitely get your money’s worth here! Before we left, the manager even stopped us at checkout to make sure everything was great and that we were satisfied with our experience.

We absolutely recommend asking for Chin as your waiter and enjoying breakfast with a beautiful beach view while watching the waves. We will definitely be back!

id: 6eab95aa-9b46-4818-9dca-54e4cea584f6
entity_slug: island-pancake-house
reviewer_name: Nichole Hudson
rating: 5
body: We had such a great experience this morning at Island Pancake House! Our waiter, Chin, was absolutely amazing. He was so polite, attentive, and made sure our family was well taken care of the entire time.

One thing that really stood out to us was that they were playing worship music this Sunday morning. As a Christian household, that meant a lot to us since we missed church this morning to spend time together as a family. When we stopped to pray over our food, Chin patiently waited for us to finish before speaking so he wouldn’t interrupt our prayer, and that small act of respect honestly meant so much.

The food was incredible too. Everything was seasoned perfectly, tasted fresh, and the portions were huge for the price. You definitely get your money’s worth here! Before we left, the manager even stopped us at checkout to make sure everything was great and that we were satisfied with our experience.

We absolutely recommend asking for Chin as your waiter and enjoying breakfast with a beautiful beach view while watching the waves. We will definitely be back!
